  <div class="clearfix container-xl tmp-px-5 tmp-mt-4 page" data-hpc="true" id="wiki-wrapper">
    <div class="d-flex flex-column flex-md-row gh-header">
      <h1 class="flex-auto min-width-0 mb-2 mb-md-0 mr-0 mr-md-2 gh-header-title">List of Prefixes for SPARQL Queries</h1>

        <div class="mt-0 mt-lg-1 flex-shrink-0 gh-header-actions">
          <a href="#wiki-pages-box" class="d-md-none ">Jump to bottom</a>

        </div>
    </div>

      <div class="mt-2 mt-md-1 tmp-pb-3 gh-header-meta">
        Liam Pond edited this page <relative-time datetime="2026-07-06T21:48:33Z" class="no-wrap">Jul 6, 2026</relative-time>
        &middot;
        <a href="/DDMAL/linkedmusic-datalake/wiki/List-of-Prefixes-for-SPARQL-Queries/_history" data-view-component="true" class="Link Link--underline">15 revisions</a>
      </div>

    <div id="wiki-content" class="tmp-mt-4">
      <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout Layout--flowRow-until-md Layout--sidebarPosition-end Layout--sidebarPosition-flowRow-end">
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Layout-main">          <div id="wiki-body" class="gollum-markdown-content">
              <div class="markdown-body">
                <p>The following is a list of prefixes that can be used in SPARQL queries without needing to be explicitly defined. Virtuoso also has a list of standard RDF prefixes that aren't included in this list but that also don't need to be explicitly defined.</p>
<p>One thing to keep in mind is that slashes (<code>/</code>) cannot be used with prefixes, so things like <code>diamm:archives/12</code> or <code>diamm/archives:12</code> are invalid.</p>
<p>For TheSession, URIs for tunebooks are of the form <code>https://thesession.org/members/{user_id}/sets/{set_id}</code>, thus making prefixes unusable for this entity type. Likewise, UTSI's song URIs are query-string identifiers of the form <code>https://databases.lib.utk.edu/songdb/songdb.php?word=0&amp;title=…</code> and cannot be abbreviated with a prefix.</p>
<p>For instructions on how to add prefixes, see <a href="https://github.com/DDMAL/linkedmusic-datalake/wiki/Virtuoso-Setup-Guide#1-add-the-name-space-prefix-to-facilitate-the-sparql-query">this section</a> of the Virtuoso setup guide.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Graph Names</h2><a id="user-content-graph-names" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Graph Names" href="#graph-names"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The following prefixes may be used when selecting graphs to query within. Clicking a graph's name in the table jumps to its entity-specific prefixes further down this page.</p>
<p>Usage: When defining a graph in SPARQL using the <code>GRAPH</code> keyword, you may use a prefix instead of manually defining a graph.
For example:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="SELECT DISTINCT ?s ?p ?o
WHERE {
  GRAPH ts: {
    ?s ?p ?o .
  }
}
LIMIT 100
"><pre class="notranslate"><code>SELECT DISTINCT ?s ?p ?o
WHERE {
  GRAPH ts: {
    ?s ?p ?o .
  }
}
LIMIT 100

</code></pre></div>

<td><a href="https://db.simssa.ca/persons/" rel="nofollow">https://db.simssa.ca/persons/</a></td>
<td>Person</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>sis:</code></td>
<td><a href="https://db.simssa.ca/sources/" rel="nofollow">https://db.simssa.ca/sources/</a></td>
<td>Source</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>sit:</code></td>
<td><a href="https://db.simssa.ca/types/" rel="nofollow">https://db.simssa.ca/types/</a></td>
<td>Type</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">University of Tennessee Song Index</h3><a id="user-content-university-of-tennessee-song-index" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: University of Tennessee Song Index" href="#university-of-tennessee-song-index"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>UTSI has no entity-URI prefix: its subjects are query-string identifiers of the form <code>https://databases.lib.utk.edu/songdb/songdb.php?word=0&amp;title=&lt;TITLE&gt;</code>, which cannot be abbreviated. Use the graph prefix <code>utsi:</code> to scope queries and match on the full URIs.</p>
